# Roller platen

*Paper and carriage feature · code `PAP:platen-roller`*

Roller platen

**1867 patents**, 1852–1950. Often together with:

- **Selection:** [Full keyboard, a key per character](../features/sel-keyboard-full.md) (272)
- **Typebars:** [Typebars](../features/elem-typebar.md) (275)
- **Impression:** [Element struck to the platen](../features/imp-element-to-platen.md) (265)
- **Spacing and escapement:** [Pawl-and-rack escapement](../features/esc-pawl-rack.md) (429)
- **Paper and carriage:** [Line spacing](../features/pap-line-space.md) (744), [Paper table and guides](../features/pap-paper-guide.md) (401), [Feed rollers and paper release](../features/pap-feed-rollers.md) (284)
- **Frame and body:** [Adjustment and wear take-up](../features/frm-adjustment-wear.md) (284)

**Machines:** [Blickensd. Electric 1](../machines/blickensd-electric-1.md) (9), [Blickensderfer 5 (B)](../machines/blickensderfer-5-b.md) (8), [Blickensderfer 7](../machines/blickensderfer-7.md) (8), [Yost 15 - 19](../machines/yost-15-19.md) (8), [Blickensderfer 2](../machines/blickensderfer-2.md) (7), [Blickensderfer 3](../machines/blickensderfer-3.md) (7), [Blickensderfer 4](../machines/blickensderfer-4.md) (7), [Blickensderfer 5 (A)](../machines/blickensderfer-5-a.md) (7), [Dactyle](../machines/dactyle.md) (7), [Caligraph 3](../machines/caligraph-3.md) (6), [Caligraph 4](../machines/caligraph-4.md) (6), [Caligraph 5](../machines/caligraph-5.md) (6), [Caligraph 6, 7](../machines/caligraph-6-7.md) (6), [Bar-Lock 8, 9](../machines/bar-lock-8-9.md) (6), [Bar-Lock 10, 11](../machines/bar-lock-10-11.md) (6)

| [US499717](../patents/US499717.md) | 1893 | Crass, Frederick L. | A revolver concentric with the platen, slid by the carriage-return lever and turned by a spiral slot cam, carries a spring pawl that engages the platen's end ratchet only during the stroke. |

| [US1160504](../patents/US1160504.md) | 1915 | Gabrielson, Carl | A card holder rocks with the platen shaft for line spacing while a broad ribbon runs around a free cylindrical platen behind the card, advanced by a one-way ratchet and gravity pawl. |

| [US1162192](../patents/US1162192.md) | 1915 | Ronchetti, Joseph Albert | Quiet line-spacing mechanism with a pawl and ratchet and a release for the platen roll, plus an alining scale with adjustable support so the platen registers with the lines and frees for universal spacing. |
| [US1162434](../patents/US1162434.md) | 1915 | Anderson, Nils H. | Line-spacing mechanism with a small number of simple parts, an actuator guided on the platen roller axle, and a platen release lever, made quiet and able to space the platen by different numbers of lines. |
| [US1162507](../patents/US1162507.md) | 1915 | Petermann, Otto | Each paper finger rides on a slotted square bar in a spring-loaded two-part holder whose dog snaps into a slot and is released by pressing with one finger. |
| [US1163563](../patents/US1163563.md) | 1915 | Schneeloch, August | A loose cam disk with notches sits beside a ratchet on the platen shaft; a spring-held sliding pawl rides the disk cams and engages the ratchet teeth only at the cut-away portion, so the operator's knob sets the number of line spaces or disengages the spacing. |
| [US1163774](../patents/US1163774.md) | 1915 | Rouillot, Victor B. | Ribbon spools sit beyond the ends of the platen on a rear shaft, with the ribbon carried forward in a swinging guide frame whose bar also spaces and tears the work-sheets, and a clutch frame picks which spool the line-space ratchet winds. |
| [US1163790](../patents/US1163790.md) | 1915 | Stickney, Burnham C. | An internal gear on the line-space wheel meshes with pinions on a fixed arm keyed to the platen axle and on two loose arms; ratchets on the pinions lock the loose arms to the fixed arm so there is no relative rotation, and the platen can still be released by hand. |
| [US1163971](../patents/US1163971.md) | 1915 | Clark, Edward L. C. | The paper feed rollers that bear on the platen roller can all be moved out of engagement together by one operator action, so the sheet can be straightened or adjusted during the job. |
| [US1164942](../patents/US1164942.md) | 1915 | Kidder, Wellington Parker | The first typebar struck against the paper pushes the platen back to the right printing position, and a lock then holds it there until the operator releases it. |
| [US1164945](../patents/US1164945.md) | 1915 | Lorenz, William A. | The platen can be moved relative to the printing plane to take the blow of the type bars through several sheets, is held fixed after adjustment, and a gage measures sheet thickness. |
| [US1165547](../patents/US1165547.md) | 1915 | Sholes, Zalmon G. | The spacing ratchet sits on a hollow conical head that revolves freely on the platen shaft within a ring, with a handle-driven pin in a groove, so no spreading pressure reaches the frame sides and the platen turns with equal ease. |
| [US1166390](../patents/US1166390.md) | 1915 | Sholes, Zalmon G. | A light platen whose body is ground pith (corn stalk, rush or sunflower stem) bound with printers' roll gelatine that is poured in hot around a core and covered with rubber, so it is quiet and light. |
| [JP30202](../patents/JP30202.md) | 1916 | Sugimoto, Kyōta | A frame carrying a large paper cylinder and a lever that lifts one loose type out of a cell magazine, inks it and strikes it on the paper, with automatic character spacing from the same lever and added line-space, clamp and spring-adjust devices. |
| [DE307964](../patents/DE307964.md) | 1916 |  | A second feed roller for the original sheet is geared to the main platen feed, so both move together at a chosen ratio, with an intermediate gear that a hand lever disengages for setting the original. |
| [DE294395](../patents/DE294395.md) | 1916 | Underwood, Typewriter Co | A crank drives a pawl and ratchet on the platen axle, and an adjustable stop on a disk that can rotate about the platen axis limits the angle, so one crank movement turns the platen more than a full revolution. |
| [JP31977](../patents/JP31977.md) | 1917 | Shinozawa, Yūsaku | A single handle moved over a flat chart board turns a large type drum (forward-back) and slides the paper-cylinder carriage along the drum (left-right), so the pointer position on the chart maps to the type at the print point. |
| [JP33067](../patents/JP33067.md) | 1918 | Shimada, Minokichi | Type slugs with a large type on one face and a small type on the other are hooked into spring-tensioned chains in a pivoted frame, so any one slug can be unhooked and swapped and the frame flipped for the other size. |
| [USRE14495](../patents/USRE14495.md) | 1918 | Latta, Emmit G. | The frame is split into a stationary lower section holding type bars and keys and a movable section carrying carriage, platen and ribbon that lowers into it, so the machine collapses to about the height of the keyboard. |
| [JP34910](../patents/JP34910.md) | 1919 | Shimada, Minokichi | A holding frame moved under a character index plate by a handle and pointer prints from a type frame, with upper and lower storage frames for the common and rare type frames that can be swapped into the holding frame by a button. |
| [USRE14599](../patents/USRE14599.md) | 1919 | Petermann, Otto | A separable support lets the carriage and platen shift rearward into compact relation to the frame when not in use, lowering the machine height. |
| [JP34472](../patents/JP34472.md) | 1919 | Sugimoto, Kyōta | Cover the sliding type magazine with a fixed index plate of the same characters and add a pointer that follows the magazine forward and back and the frame left and right, so the pointer always shows which type is at the printing point. |
| [JP37752](../patents/JP37752.md) | 1919 | Uwagawa, Atsushi | Pressing a handle lifts a loose two-character type from a tray into a swinging seal cylinder, which then turns to print it on a roller; a turn of the handle head changes lift height to choose the upper or lower character. |
| [JP34924](../patents/JP34924.md) | 1919 | Shimada, Minokichi | A pair of rocking levers joined by a detachable hook, plus a bell-crank piece driving a push bar and rack, lets the same print and space buttons either rotate the cylinder or traverse it. |
| [JP43693](../patents/JP43693.md) | 1919 | Katō, Kenjirō | Pivot the feed pawl loosely on the pawl lever and add a knob-turned springy stop piece that either locks the pawl rigid or leaves it free to tilt, so it engages later on the return stroke and feeds fewer teeth. |
| [JP37919](../patents/JP37919.md) | 1919 | Shimada, Minokichi | A hook hanging from an upper lever on the cylinder frame engages a lower key-driven lever, and a spring-loaded pin on the hook drives a ratchet and gear on a rack so one key stroke traverses the paper cylinder one character. |
| [JP38396](../patents/JP38396.md) | 1920 | Spiro, Charles | A rock shaft with a pin moves a line-space lever carrying a feed pawl; an adjustable gauge hook limits the stroke to set the line space, and the pawl is drawn clear of the platen ratchet at rest so the platen lifts out freely. |
| [JP39836](../patents/JP39836.md) | 1920 | Yoshimi, Masao | Keep all characters right-reading on one flat stereotype-like plate, move a pointer frame over it by hand, and print through an endless transfer band that takes ink from the type face and deposits it on the paper cylinder. |
| [DE320665](../patents/DE320665.md) | 1920 | J, Bruening & Sohn Akt Ges | A platen core of plywood, several veneers wound with alternating grain, gives even elasticity in all radial directions so only a thin rubber layer is needed. |
| [DE320666](../patents/DE320666.md) | 1920 | Bertrand, Otto | A portable hand grinder with a side frame, clamping jaws, lead screw and crank holds the platen between centres so it can be ground in place. |
| [DE322032](../patents/DE322032.md) | 1920 |  | A conical friction dome on the roller axis; spring-loaded levers press the ratchet wheel rim against the dome, released by a push button. |
| [DE322150](../patents/DE322150.md) | 1920 | Enders, Robert | A single hand-swung frame drives, through an adjustable angle lever and a tilting beam, the strike of the selected type lever and the carriage advance together. |
| [DE322663](../patents/DE322663.md) | 1920 |  | A slide moved by the crank pulls the pressure roller frame, and a changeover means, operated by the totalizing key, moves the roller to the part of the swing frame that suits item printing or total printing. |
| [DE322748](../patents/DE322748.md) | 1920 | Und, Mercedes Bureau Maschinen | An inclined slot on the adjustment handle makes turning the handle trigger the clutch, so one hand both rotates the platen and releases it. |
| [DE322750](../patents/DE322750.md) | 1920 | Wasem, Hermann | Slotted side cheeks take the platen axle; spring-loaded bearing bushes seat in conical recesses so the platen lifts out and seats without tools. |
| [DE324213](../patents/DE324213.md) | 1920 | Winklhofer, Wanderer Werke Vorm | A radially sliding wedge, pushed by weak springs into the slot of a split coupling ring, is pulled out by hand with a release pin so the ring only has to leave the ring gear. |
| [DE325078](../patents/DE325078.md) | 1920 | Winklhofer, Wanderer Werke Vorm | A shift rod lifts the clutch pawl and holds it until the next forward platen rotation sets it against a housing wall, so reverse rotation has no effect on the display. |
| [DE326966](../patents/DE326966.md) | 1920 | Birsner, Franz | Two brake levers and two locking levers share one rotating piece so that brake and lock alternate, never acting together, with equal spring pressure on both ends of the platen. |
| [DE329782](../patents/DE329782.md) | 1920 | Co, Remington Typewriter | An adjustment device on the roller frame sets the platen so both ends are at the same distance from the type-lever stop surface. |
| [DE334712](../patents/DE334712.md) | 1921 | Furtwaengle, Uhrenfabrik Vorm L | Radial pins pushed out by a cone bear on the middle of balls arranged around them, so the balls clamp the line-space wheel to the platen axle much more firmly. |
| [DE335769](../patents/DE335769.md) | 1921 | Ag, Seidel & Naumann | A pawl in the platen axis latches a control rod in the disengaged position, so a single push button moving the rod one way or the other alone disengages or engages the clutch. |
| [DE335770](../patents/DE335770.md) | 1921 | Winklhofer, Wanderer Werke Vorm | The pawl is guided by a slot so it engages the line-space wheel before the slide moves and leaves it only after the stroke ends, so the platen is always held and the return is quiet. |
| [DE335766](../patents/DE335766.md) | 1921 | Ag, Vorm Seidel & Naumann | Locking members on the platen frame carry a pin that sits against the carriage frame in rest position, and they are released only by the changeover roller, so the platen falls back smoothly with little pivoting or friction. |
| [DE336672](../patents/DE336672.md) | 1921 | Co, Remington Typewriter | A single link carrying both the reversing (sliding) feed pawl and a lock that keeps the feed rack engaged is attached to the oscillating shaft and shifts with the push rod and paper carriage. |
| [DE336675](../patents/DE336675.md) | 1921 | Furtwaengle, Uhrenfabrik Vorm L | Multi-layer leaf-spring shock absorbers wrap the platen pivot pins; air gaps between layers damp the transmitted noise. |
| [DE338613](../patents/DE338613.md) | 1921 | Furtwaengle, Uhrenfabrik Vorm L | Bearing bodies fully enclose the roll journals, held by a slip-over ring or a snap pawl, so the roll stays put and is exchangeable. |
| [DE338614](../patents/DE338614.md) | 1921 | Koehler, Max | Mount all ribbon parts, including the spools, on the vertically sliding shift frame so the whole ribbon device rises and falls with the paper carriage. |
| [DE342370](../patents/DE342370.md) | 1921 |  | A lever engages the paper-advance drive through a sector and plate, advancing the tape to show the printout and returning it by the same amount. |
| [DE345367](../patents/DE345367.md) | 1921 | Ges, Fried Krupp Akt | The switching lever and lifting lever are arranged with a weaker stop spring so that the paper roller can only be switched and held when the switching lever is in its effective position, preventing accidental switching. |
| [JP67155](../patents/JP67155.md) | 1921 | Kitagawa, Tōkichi | A large rotating multi-row character disc is carried on a centre shaft that also slides along a slot, so disc rotation plus shaft travel bring any character under a printing-hole pointer, where pressing prints it onto paper wrapped round a cylinder. |
| [DE345368](../patents/DE345368.md) | 1921 | B, Titania Schreibmaschinen G M | The paper roller can be uncoupled from the switching drive at either end by counter-gear levers or rollers, with simple lathe-made parts and a punched frame for cheap production. |
| [DE345671](../patents/DE345671.md) | 1921 | Ag, Triumph Werke Nuernberg | The platen axis is split into two parts joined by a tube; a spring-loaded sliding sleeve with eccentric-driven pawls acts as a freewheel, so the platen can be uncoupled and interchanged. |
| [DE346705](../patents/DE346705.md) | 1922 | Gagliazzo, Carlo | The carriage carrier is a bracket with hooks that hang on cross bars of the machine stand, so the carriage can be lifted off, and the paper-roller frame is carried by arms so it detaches with the rollers as a whole. |
| [DE346711](../patents/DE346711.md) | 1922 | Sandor, Franz | The paper roller frame is carried on support arms and can be lifted freely from the switching device at any carriage position, so it can swing up at any moment for edge setting or erasing. |
| [DE346815](../patents/DE346815.md) | 1922 | Winklhofer, Wanderer Werke Vorm | The platen or its switching frame is connected to a body under spring action so that the lever arm of the spring tension shortens as the spring tension grows and lengthens as it drops, so the platen weight is balanced and the key effort is constant in both directions. |
| [DE346817](../patents/DE346817.md) | 1922 | Co, Underwood Typewriter | A device for laying several sheets on the platen so they register without side perforations, letting narrow sheets be inserted and aligned quickly. The excerpt read does not show the full mechanism. |
| [DE346816](../patents/DE346816.md) | 1922 | Winklhofer, Wanderer Werke Vorm | Square guide stones are pivotably mounted in bushings fixed to the platen frame side panels, so manufacturing misalignment is compensated and the stones wear less. |
| [DE346818](../patents/DE346818.md) | 1922 | Co, Underwood Typewriter | A pivoting paper table or guide lifts the webs off part of the platen so they bend through only a small arc (about 40 to 60 degrees), loosening them so carbon sheets can be withdrawn. |
| [DE347699](../patents/DE347699.md) | 1922 | Leidenroth, Gustav | A key lever and bell-crank linkage with a roller and a notched leaf spring sets the ribbon carrier to a few fixed lift heights, and the set position is recognisable from the key. |
| [DE348156](../patents/DE348156.md) | 1922 | Leidenroth, Gustav | A ball-type clutch between line-switch wheel and paper roller is disengaged by a separate control organ on the hollow shaft, so the roller can be adjusted independently, and re-engages for line-by-line switching. |
| [DE348157](../patents/DE348157.md) | 1922 | Inc, Royal Typewriter Co | Coupling teeth on the platen and the switching member are inclined so they slide into engagement and reach full depth only at the end of the shift, letting the parts self-seat in gaps when slightly misaligned. |
| [DE348775](../patents/DE348775.md) | 1922 | Ges, Clemens Mueller Akt | Radial pins in the handwheel rim, with cones on their inner ends, push a coupling part axially to release the line-space wheel; pressing the pins alone frees and turns the platen, and releasing re-couples it automatically. |
| [DE348776](../patents/DE348776.md) | 1922 | Sandor, Franz | A control sleeve on the platen axis is turned by a handle and moves axially by a wedge tooth and abutment, holding the line-index wheel decoupled without a hand on the button, and it is friction-locked. |
| [DE348777](../patents/DE348777.md) | 1922 | Ges, Clemens Mueller Akt | A bracket carries a spring-loaded cone that engages notches for the writing and tilted positions, adjusting screws set the writing position, and a turn of the bracket releases the carrier for removal. |
| [DE351090](../patents/DE351090.md) | 1922 | Sandor, Franz | The roller frame bearings are cylindrical bushings rotating in small housings with eccentric bores, so turning them moves the roller frame to set the correct distance to the type. |
| [DE351658](../patents/DE351658.md) | 1922 | Co, Burroughs Adding Machine | A lever-driven paper roller drive with a settable stop gives a large adjustable line advance and a quick return feed. |
| [DE351949](../patents/DE351949.md) | 1922 | Ges, Clemens Mueller Akt | Locking bars with circular bolts and deeper guide brackets let the paper roller frame move vertically with low friction and be removed as a unit. |
| [DE352497](../patents/DE352497.md) | 1922 | Co, Underwood Typewriter | Type images are arranged to print upright on horizontally rotating platen lines, so the writer can read the text from the front, for Chinese script. |
| [JP43534](../patents/JP43534.md) | 1922 | Matsumoto, Eiichi | Rows of loose-type cells are gathered into a cylindrical magazine that turns and slides sideways on rails; the selected row is pushed out and a key-driven catching lever picks the single loose type from its cell, grips it and prints it on the paper. |
| [DE354034](../patents/DE354034.md) | 1922 | Co, Burroughs Adding Machine | A divided pressure roller has one part turning continuously to feed the control strip while the other part oscillates to load and eject the wage bags, and line spacing is applied only every second work cycle. |
| [DE356969](../patents/DE356969.md) | 1922 | Co, Underwood Typewriter | The platen frame is made to swing to two positions, which lets both lower and upper case be written and carbon sheets be withdrawn. |
| [DE358206](../patents/DE358206.md) | 1922 | Corp, Gourland Typewriter | The roller axle is notched at both ends and seated in bearings on slotted end plates, locked by hinged arms on a paper-setting scale, one arm engaging a groove in the axle and the other resting on it. |
| [DE358207](../patents/DE358207.md) | 1922 | Co, Underwood Typewriter | Auxiliary feed rollers grip the collection sheet only when the platen is raised, and advance it one way between letters. |
| [DE358208](../patents/DE358208.md) | 1922 | Winklhofer, Wanderer Werke Vorm | Pressure-roller arms are mounted on a separate axle in a small frame that installs as a whole, independent of the platen frame. |
| [DE359211](../patents/DE359211.md) | 1922 | Roller, Ideal | A roller with an elastic core and a tough outer layer of vulcanized vegetable oils, formed by casting in a rotating mould so the material settles on the inner wall. |
| [DE359608](../patents/DE359608.md) | 1922 | Payne, Halbert Edwin | Most of the type band's travel to the platen is done by swinging its holder, so the hammer only has to flex the band slightly at the print point. |
| [DE359980](../patents/DE359980.md) | 1922 | Ges, Fried Krupp Akt | Curved surfaces on a roller lever keep the link in contact with the lever during the switch, and a leaf spring adds damping. |
| [DE359983](../patents/DE359983.md) | 1922 | Graff, Maximilian | A spring-loaded clutch on the shift lever shaft, with a nose snapping into numbered grooves, sets the line spacing using only three parts. |
| [DE359984](../patents/DE359984.md) | 1922 | Co, Underwood Typewriter | The platen is split into two independent part rollers, each in a liftable frame that pivots up with its sheets, so one set of sheets can be loosened and withdrawn while the other is written on. |
| [DE360572](../patents/DE360572.md) | 1922 | Corp, Gourland Typewriter | A line-space shift lever mounted below the roller axis, driven by a shaft with a stop pin, leaves the roller removable without disturbing the mechanism. |
| [DE360733](../patents/DE360733.md) | 1922 | Ges, Polygraphische | Individual type sticks of varying widths are stored in magazines, brought out to form the line, and spaces are inserted between words to fill the line length. |
| [DE364325](../patents/DE364325.md) | 1922 | Weinsziehr, Jean | A springy type comb carrying several types per tine is positioned by hand in two axes under a pressure lever; one stroke of the single print lever presses the tine, spaces the carriage one tooth and advances the ribbon. |
| [DE364326](../patents/DE364326.md) | 1922 | Inc, Royal Typewriter Co | An angular auxiliary lever joined to the shift key lever by an adjustable spring lets the key overtravel without straining the carriage lift. |
| [DE365592](../patents/DE365592.md) | 1922 | Co, Underwood Typewriter | Each pressure-roller spring is tensioned by its own worm-gear adjuster, with the worm-gear parts mounted so the same parts can be made in pairs, and the rollers are carried on oscillating arms supported on the platen frame. |
| [JP45118](../patents/JP45118.md) | 1922 | Kataoka, Kōtarō | One control lever, moved over a character index plate, both selects the type (left/right turns the type cylinder by cords, forward/back moves the paper cylinder along the cylinder) and, pressed down, rocks the paper cylinder onto the type to print. |
| [DE366620](../patents/DE366620.md) | 1923 | Co, Underwood Typewriter | The platen is carried in a subframe that lifts it off the feed rollers; a clamp on the platen-holding latch presses a holding device onto the collating sheet while the carbons are withdrawn, with guide fingers that steer the paper web. |
| [DE366623](../patents/DE366623.md) | 1923 | Schneider, Hermann | Keys on a lazy-tongs (Nuremberg scissors) linkage drive types through springy wires in flexible tubes, and the platen and carriage fold in half on hinges. |
| [DE367698](../patents/DE367698.md) | 1923 | Luley, Philipp | A toothed disc with a stop is fitted to the platen axis, and a claw coupling lets the platen be turned back and forth by the letterhead height, adaptable to typewriters of any system. |
| [DE368216](../patents/DE368216.md) | 1923 | Reisser, Adolf | Friction clamping jaws are pressed against a coupling sleeve by lever pairs with long actuating arms, driven by wedge-shaped displacer rods pushed between the lever ends. |
| [JP50376](../patents/JP50376.md) | 1923 | Katō, Kenjirō | A single handle-turned change-over decides whether pressing the print lever advances the paper cylinder around (vertical feed) or along (horizontal feed) its axis, and which way the horizontal pawl pushes. |
| [DE369304](../patents/DE369304.md) | 1923 | Graff, Maximilian | A rocker under the key lever extensions completes the shift travel to a stop when any key is struck, through a slide and angle lever on the shift rod. |
| [DE369306](../patents/DE369306.md) | 1923 | Muchajer, Paul | The carriage hangs on a thin leaf spring running on rollers in a prismatic rail, so it glides without play, and a felt cushion under the carriage frame damps the impact of the types against the platen. |
| [DE371610](../patents/DE371610.md) | 1923 | Co, Underwood Typewriter | The carriage carries a sheet cart, and the paper webs rest on a firm support under their own weight, so separate webs or sheets can be written on while the carriage is relieved of pulling the paper. |
| [DE371983](../patents/DE371983.md) | 1923 | Co, Underwood Typewriter | A feed table in front of the platen has stops for the sheet edges, and the platen lifts on command (spring-assisted after a lock release) so sheets can be laid under it and aligned, then the platen is lowered; the table edges curve down to avoid creasing. |
| [JP60638](../patents/JP60638.md) | 1923 | Kataoka, Kōtarō | Many toothed type rings, each carrying a line of types on its rim, hang on a gear roller that turns as the carriage slides front to back, while one handle moves a lifting arm left and right to pick a ring and, pressed down, throws that ring against the paper cylinder. |
| [DE375794](../patents/DE375794.md) | 1923 | Co, Underwood Typewriter | A front entry plate with a stop bar on its upper edge has a holding spring clip that keeps the inserted sheet in writing position, touching only the front of the platen. |
| [DE376477](../patents/DE376477.md) | 1923 | Roebuck, Alvah Curtis | The line finder is hung in a movable articulated link on the switch frame and is joined to the guide rail by a resilient arm, so it returns with the rail without hard impacts. |
| [DE377031](../patents/DE377031.md) | 1923 | Co, Underwood Typewriter | The locking member is made adjustable on a rotatable arm, fixed by a small screw in a slot concentric with the arm pivot, so the arm sets the lock position without reworking the notched lever. |
| [DE377033](../patents/DE377033.md) | 1923 | Graff, Maximilian | A screw set outside the platen axis clamps a sleeve acting as a two-sided washer, which presses a friction disc on the platen. The clutch engages through the screw's leverage and does not shift the platen. |
| [DE380640](../patents/DE380640.md) | 1923 | Co, Underwood Typewriter | The pawl that engages the ratchet wheel is laterally adjustable and a special pawl is used for each ratchet wheel. A lever switches the working pawl, so the line pitch changes without moving the platen. |
| [DE381275](../patents/DE381275.md) | 1923 | Cerny, Jan | The keyboard prints consonants on a narrow tape in one of five offset lines, with four thumb levers shifting the platen and tape vertically by half a type height to mark the vowel. |
| [DE381277](../patents/DE381277.md) | 1923 | Co, Hooven Automatic Typewriter | A locking lever engaged between the clutch and a link, moved by a tape hole, which disengages a sliding coupling piece so the paper roller stops. |
| [DE381354](../patents/DE381354.md) | 1923 |  | The type carriage runs between an upper and lower roller in a roller frame with detachable non-load guides. |
| [DE381359](../patents/DE381359.md) | 1923 | Hundt, Otto | A rod sliding in the hollow platen axle carries a driver that engages the line wheel bore, held by springs and released by pushing a button at either end. |
| [DE381429](../patents/DE381429.md) | 1923 | Wolff, Bela | Setting keys act via key bars on one setting lever each, which move a common oscillating shaft to adjust the platen height, all in one unit with the typewriter. |
| [DE381434](../patents/DE381434.md) | 1923 | F, Naehmaschinen Und Fahrraeder | Springs are fully relaxed when the paper is released, so the feed rollers lift off the platen and the lever mechanism is set to stay unloaded. |
| [DE381435](../patents/DE381435.md) | 1923 | Ges, Weilwerke Akt | A spring bolt in the crank engages a clamping ring that is pressed onto the hand knob, coupling it to the crank for setting the platen rotation. |
| [DE381436](../patents/DE381436.md) | 1923 |  | The locking bolt for the sliding clutch disc is itself secured by a scalar bolt, so the roller can be exchanged without accidental release. |
| [DE382954](../patents/DE382954.md) | 1923 | Wachsmann, Samuel | The drive spring travels longitudinally with the carriage, so no transmission is needed, and turning the paper roller back winds the spring again; a ratchet wheel and fixed and loose pawls advance the roller on each key press. |
| [DE384112](../patents/DE384112.md) | 1923 | Co, Underwood Typewriter | Roller support arms carry lateral attachments on a rod parallel to the platen, with adjustable springs held by a lockable set screw and a spring-loaded lift lever that stops rattling. |
| [DE385565](../patents/DE385565.md) | 1923 | Hillard, Frederic Whittlesey | A locking device holds the carriage automatically at any height of its upward movement against the return spring, and a separate catch holds the raised frame at its top position. |
| [DE384805](../patents/DE384805.md) | 1923 | Beuttler, Gottlob | A double-armed lever tilts the tool so the edge continuously renews against the roller; a slotted guide inclines the lever. |
| [DE386402](../patents/DE386402.md) | 1923 | B, Elek Sche Schreibmaschinen M | Use a vernier coupling with many closely spaced pins on star-shaped springs and nested toothed discs of 33 and 32 gaps for about 1.1 mm of arc. |
| [DE386471](../patents/DE386471.md) | 1923 | Muchajer, Paul | A single drive lever, pushed down by any key, sets the type cylinder angle through a comb rail, prints, and spaces the carriage, with an optional single electromagnet assisting. |
| [DE385566](../patents/DE385566.md) | 1923 | Corp, Gourland Typewriter | The guide plate is hung above the paper roller so its guide rollers press forward and upward, on a frame carried by a tongue cut from the guide plate. |
| [DE385646](../patents/DE385646.md) | 1923 | Winklhofer, Wanderer Werke Vorm | A plain cylindrical journal carries a displaceable clamping cone that a threaded nut drives into a conical bore in the platen flange, locking the platen firmly. |
