| [DE498604](../patents/DE498604.md) | 1930 | Schmid, Max | The paper sledge's forward and backward stroke itself advances the roller carrier one hole: perforated walls on the roller carrier engage fixed tapered pins of different strength. |
| [DE498605](../patents/DE498605.md) | 1930 | Schmid, Max | The platen housing slides on cross bars of a U-section support rail that runs on machine rods, and loose brass sleeves with rubber rollers on the guide rods also feed paper. |
| [JP88112](../patents/JP88112.md) | 1930 | Kataoka, Kōtarō | A hand-turned type drum holds type cases whose faces are covered by swinging index plates; the key lifts the wanted case, flips its index plate away, and a surround and pin through the type grip it and swing it against the paper cylinder. |
| [DE500082](../patents/DE500082.md) | 1930 | Winklhofer, Wanderer Werke Vorm | The shaft ends rest on rollers between top bearings on the carriage frame, and these bearing supports are adjustable by eccentric screws to keep the platen parallel. |
| [DE500084](../patents/DE500084.md) | 1930 | Mueller, Wilhelm | A single lever lowers the type lever segment and folds the type guide, ribbon guide, line scale and paper holder away from the platen, held by a locking spring. |
| [DE500085](../patents/DE500085.md) | 1930 | Winklhofer, Wanderer Werke Vorm | A trough-shaped middle connecting rail, stiffer than cast, sheet or wire-braced versions, carries the release shaft for the paper pressure rollers and resists bending. |
| [DE502800](../patents/DE502800.md) | 1930 | Co, Underwood Elliott Fisher | Each carbon sheet sits on its own holder with a parallel guide shaft running on a fixed rack, and retraction springs pull each holder back separately. |
| [DE503144](../patents/DE503144.md) | 1930 | Wallenstein, Alfred | A stepped control body on the platen axis has wedge-shaped sliding surfaces so the one lever stroke moves the pawl axially and tangentially at once, giving smooth line feed with 1, 2 or 3 line steps selected by a hand wheel. |
| [DE505406](../patents/DE505406.md) | 1930 | Geb, Merz Merz Werke | Hinge the pawl on a pull tab that moves horizontally, itself spring-hinged to the short arm of the angled lever, so lateral play is negligible while radial movement happens at the joint. |
| [DE505898](../patents/DE505898.md) | 1930 | Pateman, Arthur | Locking hooks rest on support bearings in the platen and are guided in circumferential grooves of a non-rotating clamp member, which presses them into the toothed ring. |
| [DE506056](../patents/DE506056.md) | 1930 | G, Europa Schreibmaschinen A | Each roller floats on the guide rod by a stepped coil spring, and flat springs keep the rollers from being dragged along the rod. |
| [DE506154](../patents/DE506154.md) | 1930 | H, Adlerwerke Kleyer Ag | Platen side indentations let the rollers drop in so the knife-shaped rail lies closer to the paper, and its edge can tear strips off roll paper. |
| [DE506054](../patents/DE506054.md) | 1930 | Co, Remington Typewriter | Mount the line pawl, line lever and linkages on one support piece that folds up on a pin behind the ratchet, so the platen can be lifted out without altering the gear. |
| [DE506158](../patents/DE506158.md) | 1930 |  | A rotatable drum carries an adjustable numbering or date cliche on its circumference and has ribbon rolls inside it; a coupling sets the drum rest position so each imprint lands at a set distance from the document's leading edge against a counter-pressure roller. |
| [DE508101](../patents/DE508101.md) | 1930 | Co, Underwood Elliott Fisher | Thin carbon paper is held by guide plates in a bulge over the platen at the writing line so that it stays put, and a supply roll is housed within the bulge. |
| [DE508169](../patents/DE508169.md) | 1930 | Maschf, Rheinische Metallw & | Rotatable coupling bolts in bores through the platen hood have end faces that turn out past the roller surface to press against the inner surface of the index wheel. |
| [DE512139](../patents/DE512139.md) | 1930 | Winklhofer, Wanderer Werke Vorm | Left and right roll parts carry rulings of two different widths, with end rings whose rulings coincide with the other part, so forms of several line widths can be lined up. |
| [DE513464](../patents/DE513464.md) | 1930 | Kaddatz, Arthur | A swinging wiper rail on a square rod strips the hanging sheets off the pins and presses them against the platen when it swings out, with a rocker that releases the pressure roller. |
| [DE518614](../patents/DE518614.md) | 1931 | E, Greve John | A narrower second paper guide sits under a raised platen, and a tab key skips the paper carriage by set distances so that both sheets index together. |
| [DE519815](../patents/DE519815.md) | 1931 | Wallenstein, Alfred | Paper roller bearing tabs fit into slots of the bifurcated carriage side part and are released by a bolt, with a filler piece covering the fork; injection molding and simple sawing suffice. |
| [DE520630](../patents/DE520630.md) | 1931 | Meusel, Hans | Spring-loaded centre-punch tips on traverses at the square ends of the platen axle engage a fixed disc with holes one line apart, indexing the roller a line at a time inside an enclosing knob. |
| [DE521881](../patents/DE521881.md) | 1931 | Ag, Triumph Werke Nuernberg | A second hand lever sits next to the ordinary line-feed lever on the same pin and drives a pawl that rotates the platen backward by one line. |
| [DE524898](../patents/DE524898.md) | 1931 | G, Weilwerke A | Elastic tension members fixed to a shaft behind the platen pass under the platen and grip a double rail that holds the booking sheet, so it is set to a chosen line height independently of platen rotation and inserted from the front. |
| [DE527241](../patents/DE527241.md) | 1931 | Winklhofer, Wanderer Werke Vorm | Guide rollers are made continuous with hard inserts at the points where the pressure levers act. |
| [JP94273](../patents/JP94273.md) | 1931 | Ishii, Shōkichi | Set many rim-typed magazine discs side by side on an arc shaft, each with its own key and type bar, so that once the magazines are turned to one row, any of the types in that row can be printed one after another from the keys. |
| [DE517214](../patents/DE517214.md) | 1931 | G, Astra Werke A | A parallelogram lever linkage, a swinging slider and a rack carry the setting pointer's motion to a vertical type cylinder placed right beside the adding machine's printing unit. |
| [JP93912](../patents/JP93912.md) | 1931 | Iisaku, Takichirō | A spring-returned swinging frame carries a pitch-setting pawl slide and a locking catch, so each swing feeds the platen gear by a set number of teeth and the catch stops the gear dead at the end of the feed. |
| [JP95068](../patents/JP95068.md) | 1931 | Ishikawa, Toyoji | Hang the whole machine body on a sliding rail shaft so it tips forward on pulling the handle lever, which both exposes the printed area and lets the printing lever reach into the tilted type store to grip a type. |
| [JP94690](../patents/JP94690.md) | 1931 | Kataoka, Kōtarō | Store loose types in slanted cases with index-character covers, raise the selected case with a selector lever, pick one type onto a slanted type-holding rod, and swing the rod to the cylinder with a printing lever that also feeds the carriage. |
| [DE535580](../patents/DE535580.md) | 1931 |  | The platen and its counter roller are made movable so a loose sheet is pushed in, gripped, and then pinched between the rollers so it arrives at the print point together with the registration tape. |
| [DE536364](../patents/DE536364.md) | 1931 | Winklhofer, Wanderer Werke Vorm | The release axis carries flats; in the normal turned position both bearing arms are lifted, and when the axis is shifted axially a further flat lifts only the rear roller's bearing arm. |
| [DE536895](../patents/DE536895.md) | 1931 | Inc, Royal Typewriter Co | The movable coupling part is carried on pins fixed to the roller head and is pressed by one spring around the roller axis, so the platen roller head is not weakened and the clutch is simpler to make. |
| [JP94714](../patents/JP94714.md) | 1931 | Iisaku, Takichirō | A hand key on a swinging shaft with a cam, a delayed pawl change-over lever and a loose-rack return arm backs the carriage exactly one rack pitch per press without disturbing the forward drive. |
| [DE509940](../patents/DE509940.md) | 1931 | Weissfloch, Konrad Karl | A poster typewriter with large types on an endless cloth band moved past the print point by a pointer lever over a letter scale, struck from the front by a hammer so the writing is visible. |
| [DE538089](../patents/DE538089.md) | 1931 | Aillaud, Louis | A separate note keyboard is joined by angle levers and a flexible Bowden cable to a clamp on the platen, which twists the platen to each note position, so it works with any standard typewriter. |
| [DE539105](../patents/DE539105.md) | 1931 | Strauss, Emil | Dots are formed in a rotary press in one pass and covered with a resistant but elastic mass on both sides, giving two or four pages per revolution. |
| [JP96359](../patents/JP96359.md) | 1931 | Makabe, Masaya | Each retaining plate rides in its own spiral slot in the magazine flanges, overlapping like armour scales, and a key-driven pin on a turning disc pushes aside only the plate over the wanted type. |
| [DE540613](../patents/DE540613.md) | 1931 | Sebastian, Friedmann | A slotted clamping ring between the line-spacing wheel and the platen cover is spread by the flats on the coupling bolt's end web, so the wheel keeps its round shape and the clutch engages with little rotation. |
| [DE542356](../patents/DE542356.md) | 1932 | Lipp, Karl | A thin, pliable base sheet, softer than the types but harder than the platen, carries sharp-edged corrugations formed from its own material; they cut the paper where the types strike. |
| [DE545352](../patents/DE545352.md) | 1932 | Ges, Weilwerke Akt | A second paper table on articulated arms is hinged behind the normal table; it pivots into position with pressure rollers that press onto the platen and can be folded back. |
| [DE545922](../patents/DE545922.md) | 1932 | Sa, Stenotype Grandjean | A one-piece pawl with a switching tooth and a trailing safety tooth, plus an adjustable stop on the holder, moves the ratchet exactly one tooth per stroke. |
| [DE547357](../patents/DE547357.md) | 1932 | G, Weilwerke A | A guardrail on swing arms is pivoted on forward-hinged support arms carrying small rollers against the platen, and folds away out of sight. |
| [DE548378](../patents/DE548378.md) | 1932 | H, Taylorix Organisation G M B | An endless band passes around an extra roller and the platen, holding the account sheet by itself, with a second alignment device on the roller support. |
| [DE549651](../patents/DE549651.md) | 1932 | Lipp, Carl | The corrugated hollow cylinder sector is clamped or spring-loaded to the platen, covering more than half its circumference, and can be fitted and removed easily. |
| [DE551077](../patents/DE551077.md) | 1932 | G, Europa Schreibmaschinen A | A line guide plate carries several paper guide tongues spread across the platen width, with adjustable stops, so sheets pass under the paper guide rod while it stays in pressure position and stiff cards are still guided. |
| [DE551171](../patents/DE551171.md) | 1932 | Kraus, Hans | A self-contained closed box holds the drive and an eccentric axle couples to any paper-roller axis; an internal-tooth gear and an adjustable stop limit the advance and lock the line position. |
| [DE551518](../patents/DE551518.md) | 1932 | H, Adlerwerke Kleyer Ag | The platen is held stationary and separately from the stop mechanism, which moves along it in prismatic guides with rollers; the form tapes run from fixed roll holders over the platen. |
| [DE552610](../patents/DE552610.md) | 1932 | Winklhofer, Wanderer Werke Vorm | Three holding members mounted on rails above the platen fold down at the back, with pressure rollers and support plates that can be swung out of the way so the sheets are clamped independently and exchanged quickly. |
| [DE552608](../patents/DE552608.md) | 1932 | Co, Dictatype Shorthand Machine | A paper gripper rides on the platen axle with play and is driven by a handlebar from an angle lever, so one tension spring both advances the paper and drops the keys. |
| [DE553690](../patents/DE553690.md) | 1932 | Kaeppler, Paul | Carriage guide rails in the lower part are adjusted against each other by opposing screws bearing on support arms, giving a fine, lockable platen-to-type setting. |
| [DE554883](../patents/DE554883.md) | 1932 | Bechmann, Hans | The paper spools are mounted in the carriage so they slide radially and are pressed against the platen by elastic means, so the web feeds without extra coupling gears or feathers. |
| [DE555529](../patents/DE555529.md) | 1932 | Ge, Europa Schreibmaschinen Akt | A second pinch-roller set spanning the full platen width sits on a front rail that swings down to align sheets, linked to the paper guide rod so lifting the rod swings the rail onto the platen. |
| [DE555947](../patents/DE555947.md) | 1932 | Pasqualini, Don Guido | A second adjustment keyboard drives a lever linkage with a changeable ratio to the platen rotation, so line spacing can be set to the staff width. |
| [DE556370](../patents/DE556370.md) | 1932 | Werke, Mercedes Bueromaschinen | The lift linkage stays in constant frictional or positive connection with the power drive, with an eccentric drive disengaged at dead centres, for slow noiseless platen movement. |
| [DE558756](../patents/DE558756.md) | 1932 | Co, Dictatype Shorthand Machine | Advance the paper by teeth acting on the paper itself, which turns the platen by friction, and take the ribbon drive from the platen rotation through a planetary gear on a swivel arm. |
| [DE562589](../patents/DE562589.md) | 1932 | Inhoffen, Walther | A magnet armature moves an actuator that acts through wedge surfaces on two oppositely movable toothed racks, one of which is stopped by the struck key, so the cylinder is turned by the difference of travel. |
| [DE564219](../patents/DE564219.md) | 1932 | Lewin, Awram | Mount the key set on a slider, with keys whose heads span two type levers, so shifting the slider makes the keys strike either one type set or both at once. |
| [DE568010](../patents/DE568010.md) | 1933 | Inc, Royal Typewriter Co | The platen pressure abutment has two pressure faces of different hardness (hard metal and soft rubber insert) and is reversible or adjustable on its support arms so either face can be brought to the type. |
| [DE568788](../patents/DE568788.md) | 1933 | Co, Remington Typewriter | A releasable stop limits the adjusting crank in normal use; a special handle disengages it so the adjustment can go past the normal limit, and it snaps back to the locked position afterwards. |
| [DE569363](../patents/DE569363.md) | 1933 | H, Adlerwerke Kleyer Ag | The platen frame pivots on the machine frame and a stop mechanism steps the frame along the roller; spring fingers with pressure rollers hold the forms and swing out for quick exchange, with a collector for spoiled forms. |
| [DE570164](../patents/DE570164.md) | 1933 | Co, Remington Typewriter | The card slot leads into a cavity inside the roller, with adjustable depth stops and resilient holders, so card tongues stay inside the roller. |
| [DE570275](../patents/DE570275.md) | 1933 | Winklhofer, Wanderer Werke Vorm | Both rails pivot on axes in front of the platen and fold upward, so arbitrary-width sheets can be exchanged in one operation. |
| [JP101907](../patents/JP101907.md) | 1933 | Kawashima, Saichirō | An adjustable inclined plate in the path of the spring-loaded feed pawl holds the pawl off the rack during part of its stroke, so that the pawl feeds either one tooth or its full stroke of teeth depending on the plate's set position. |
| [DE573912](../patents/DE573912.md) | 1933 | Co, Remington Typewriter | A separate spindle, rotatable by the pin and the platen and arranged in the platen, opens and closes the centring chuck from the outside, so the chuck is worked without turning the parts it grips. |
| [DE574082](../patents/DE574082.md) | 1933 | Inc, Royal Typewriter Co | The writing bow and the writing abutment are shifted relative to each other before line feed, releasing the grip so the form can move without dragging the copies; platen, form and carbon movements are driven by one common lever. |
| [JP102040](../patents/JP102040.md) | 1933 | Shimizu, Jirō | Notched cams turning with the paper cylinder automatically pick a stop pin to limit the cylinder's return at each line, and pick a stepped stop to limit a release-lever run for wide gaps. |
| [JP103518](../patents/JP103518.md) | 1933 | Suganuma, Seiichi | Split the platen's printing face into a left and a right area, and let a bar moving with the platen carriage rock a lever so that only the vertical feed or only the horizontal feed engages, depending on which area is at the print point. |
| [DE577949](../patents/DE577949.md) | 1933 | H, Adlerwerke Kleyer Ag | A stop on the frame and a counter-stop on the roller frame make the frame return to a fixed position where the first line of the form sits in the writing line; height stops set the position of loose sheets. |
| [DE580715](../patents/DE580715.md) | 1933 | Marchthal, Eduard | A tensile-stress means acting on the free end of the paper keeps the sheet under constant tension at the impression point against the feed mechanism, removing play in the paper feed. |
| [DE581078](../patents/DE581078.md) | 1933 | Co, Remington Typewriter | Put the line feed ratchet arm on a horizontal axis behind the roller knob, with the lever joined to it so it folds down beside the knob for packing. |
| [JP104642](../patents/JP104642.md) | 1933 | Miwa, Mataichi | A flip-over T-shaped control piece carrying the feed pawl and a trip roller, held by a toggle spring lever, drops the pawl into the wheel only at the end of a full print stroke and is flipped out again by a preset adjustable limiter, which also re-engages a brake pawl. |
| [DE582551](../patents/DE582551.md) | 1933 | Winklhofer, Wanderer Werke Vorm | The platen is wrapped with closely wound fine steel wire, ends soldered to the body so the wire rests resiliently, which embosses and cuts the paper for indelible characters. |
| [DE523698](../patents/DE523698.md) | 1933 | Krauss, Georg | Connect the needle ends through flexible Bowden cables to solenoid cores arranged in rows below. |
| [DE585069](../patents/DE585069.md) | 1933 | Werke, Mercedes Bueromaschinen | The pin-row table pivots on spring-loaded joints, releasing the sheets as it lowers and pressing them against the roller. |
| [DE586252](../patents/DE586252.md) | 1933 | Stueber, Eugen | A honeycomb key layout allows one, two or three adjacent keys to be pressed with one finger, with superposed keys sharing one print point. |
| [DE589915](../patents/DE589915.md) | 1933 | Werke, Mercedes Bueromaschinen | A spreader member pivoted in the platen's expansion ring opening is spring-pressed into the spread position, giving a compact, easily reached expanding clutch that holds with low force. |
| [DE590241](../patents/DE590241.md) | 1933 | Huellen, Josef | A friction wheel on a housing clamped to the carriage is driven by the platen knob and counts revolutions, with a scale that shows the lines; when the last line is reached a bell signal sounds, and a lever sets the speed for any sheet length. |
| [DE592209](../patents/DE592209.md) | 1934 | Typewriters, L C Smith & Corona | Ink sheet holders move in longitudinal grooves in the platen itself, clamping the paper webs and ink sheets against the roller, so the machine is no larger than an ordinary typewriter and existing machines can be converted. |
| [DE593927](../patents/DE593927.md) | 1934 | Werke, Mercedes Bueromaschinen | A platen-lift stroke longer than the switching stroke raises the paper guide devices clear of the obstructing parts so sheets can be inserted without manipulation. |
| [JP106965](../patents/JP106965.md) | 1934 | Shimizu, Jirō | Use the unwinding force of one wound spring to push cams that move the cylinder, with the pawls of the feed device holding the cams back and a dead-centre linkage letting them snap through for column change and cylinder return. |
| [JP107636](../patents/JP107636.md) | 1934 | Suganuma, Seiichi | Two facing knobs pinched together first turn the cylinder back to an adjustable stop (the level of the next column's first character), then pulling one knob further releases the carriage to slide sideways by one spacer-block interval under an alternating-knife escapement. |
| [DE595898](../patents/DE595898.md) | 1934 | Winklhofer, Wanderer Werke Vorm | A coupling pawl with wedge surfaces is moved by a lever on a common pivot whose start position is fixed by a micrometer stop, so the platen advances exactly by the set line distance. |
| [DE596961](../patents/DE596961.md) | 1934 | Haberfeld, Erwin O | A paper cup shaped and mounted so that, pressed from the front, it draws the inserted sheet taut from front to back and keeps the sheets aligned. |
| [JP107719](../patents/JP107719.md) | 1934 | Shimizu, Jirō | A back-feed key drives a cam that frees the forward pawls, turns the drive gear back with its own pawl, and re-engages a reverse holding pawl before the cam finishes, so the platen steps back an exact amount. |
| [JP108017](../patents/JP108017.md) | 1934 | Kawana, Hikoichi | Fit a friction coupling between the platen and its ratchet wheel that a screw-on release plate frees, so the platen can be turned alone by the hand wheel. |
| [JP109044](../patents/JP109044.md) | 1934 | Ōya, Takeshi | One hooked switching lever shifts the pinion bracket, a bent lever and a travel-and-rocking shaft so the printing stroke and the feed handles drive either the cylinder paper feed or the lateral feed. |
| [DE602325](../patents/DE602325.md) | 1934 | Kaeppler, Paul | A spreading lever or wedge slider on the platen shaft spreads the expansion ring to couple the line index wheel, with more secure grip than the earlier designs. |
| [DE602326](../patents/DE602326.md) | 1934 | Corp, Sundstrand | The middle roller section is turned directly by its line-feed device, and a movable coupling ties it to an end section so that all three turn as one without axial shift. |
| [DE603256](../patents/DE603256.md) | 1934 | Co, Ncr | A foldable bar along the paper roller edge, at a slot parallel to it, lets each form be set on any line without moving the others. |
| [DE605198](../patents/DE605198.md) | 1934 | Winklhofer, Wanderer Werke Vorm | Two pincer-like coupling jaws on a pivot in the line ratchet carrier are opened and closed by longitudinal movement of the platen shaft, so the platen engages the ratchet without heavy effort. |
| [DE605409](../patents/DE605409.md) | 1934 | Moussier, Leandre | A foot pedal drives a rod and cam that lift the roller smoothly, and the device fits on existing machines. |
| [JP110673](../patents/JP110673.md) | 1934 | Katō, Kenjirō | A type holder slides along the striking arm, moved by a sector and link that a coupling locks either to the frame or to the rock shaft, so the upper or lower character lands on the print point. |
| [JP110895](../patents/JP110895.md) | 1934 | Abe, Okujirō | Type cases ride on a swinging frame turned by a hand wheel, and a spring-coupled cover plate with openings slides over them to expose a chosen case or to cover all when the frame is swung fully forward. |
| [US1986244](../patents/US1986244.md) | 1935 | Bates, Harry | A swinging arm with a button is dropped onto a depression in a keyboard-shaped plate, which turns an arcuate type segment to that character, and pressing down tilts the keyboard and platen frame so the paper meets the type. |
| [DE608322](../patents/DE608322.md) | 1935 | Benzing, Hugo | A pivoting pressure piece with two clamping levers compresses the clutch plates while a weak spring is used, and the levers are forced back on release. |
| [DE609317](../patents/DE609317.md) | 1935 | Inc, Royal Typewriter Co | The writing abutment is a separate part moved independently of the switching and guiding device, so the platen is withdrawn before each line feed to loosen the sheet pack, then the sheets are advanced. |
| [DE609811](../patents/DE609811.md) | 1935 | Hoffmann, Edgar | Mount the type wheel and its gear on a slide plate guided straight against the platen in the base plate, with a fixed ratchet comb that a pin on the setting rack enters during the stroke. |
| [JP111688](../patents/JP111688.md) | 1935 | Suganuma, Seiichi | Three interlocked button levers on the spring-urged platen cylinder, caught by a sawtooth spring claw, set the cylinder to the upper, middle or lower tier when the matching button is pressed. |
| [DE612337](../patents/DE612337.md) | 1935 | Ge, Triumph Werke Nuernberg Akt | A rubber shell is pulled on the core in its unground state, cut into narrow rings at letter spacing, and then sanded, so the discs damp vibration. |
| [DE612339](../patents/DE612339.md) | 1935 | Schmidt, Ferdinand | A loose additional manuscript roller on bearing blocks seats on the carriage with a hook under the platen shaft, and its line lever rests on the platen's line lever so both can advance together or separately. |
| [DE612336](../patents/DE612336.md) | 1935 | Maul, Michael | A cutting wheel on a runner sliding along a rod rolls on a crossbar drive wheel as it traverses, so the transverse motion turns it and cuts the web that the tensioning device has stretched. |
| [JP111958](../patents/JP111958.md) | 1935 | Kawashima, Saichirō | A floating pinion alternately engages an outer and an inner gear on the platen side, so each print stroke winds one gear against a spring and the return stroke advances the platen by a fixed step. |
| [DE612499](../patents/DE612499.md) | 1935 | Conti, Walter | The roller has a rigid acid-proof shell carried on the axle or core by elastic rings, so the shell gives way under paper thickness; retaining rings keep the shell from turning or sliding. |
| [DE613630](../patents/DE613630.md) | 1935 | Co, Underwood Elliott Fisher | The line-space ratchet wheel is clamped between a fixed flange and a sliding washer, pressed by two wedge levers inside the platen end that a cam on an axially sliding shaft pushes apart. |
| [JP113896](../patents/JP113896.md) | 1935 | Kawashima, Saichirō | Both ends of one coil spring are tied to two independent movers; a pin on a platen-driven gear is set beforehand to one side or the other, so the spring's return force turns the platen a fixed step per impression in either direction. |
| [DE618378](../patents/DE618378.md) | 1935 | G, Hermann Hinz | A battery, bell and slip-ring contact are housed inside a hollow platen, and a contact roller closes the circuit when the sheet's lower edge passes, so the bell rings at the last line; the whole platen swaps between machines. |
| [DE619021](../patents/DE619021.md) | 1935 | Co, Sampson Permagraph | A small-diameter drum with ten column groups and five rows spins continuously; each key of an ordinary four-row keyboard drops a stop pin into the path of a selector arm to halt the rotation and sets a stepped lift stop for the row, so any character is chosen by one key press. |
| [JP116933](../patents/JP116933.md) | 1936 | Kawashima, Saichirō | A spiral spring strip held between rollers on a rack-moved sector, with its end on a traveller geared to a fixed rack and to the cylinder's sliding frame, gives a pull to the left or right depending on which side of the traveller the rollers are placed. |
| [DE626187](../patents/DE626187.md) | 1936 | Winklhofer, Wanderer Werke Vorm | A manually adjustable actuator with two detent grooves lifts the sensor off the sheet so the locking device can be switched off and back on. |
| [DE628803](../patents/DE628803.md) | 1936 | Winklhofer, Wanderer Werke Vorm | The angle transmission member is made in two parts, with a swivelling fork part that can be pivoted out, so the locking device releases reliably and returns freely. |
| [DE754200](../patents/DE754200.md) | 1936 | Co, Underwood Elliott Fisher | Each platen part gets its own line-switching device, normally blocked; control members on the moving carriage release the return device at chosen column positions, so each part can be switched on its own. |
| [DE635848](../patents/DE635848.md) | 1936 | Ag, Vorm Seidel & Naumann | One loosely rotatable axis serves both as the platen shift pivot and as the carriage release shaft, and a stiffening bar on the roller frame does several jobs. |
| [JP118800](../patents/JP118800.md) | 1936 | Hariu, Kiyotake | A slide that moves with the line-feed lever has its stroke limited by one of several stops, chosen by pressing a push-button that swings the stops into the slide's path. |
| [DE636870](../patents/DE636870.md) | 1936 | Winklhofer, Wanderer Werke Vorm | The pre-insertion holding rail folds down about an axis in front of the platen, bringing its leading edge close to the lower guide, and a lifting member raises the platen as the rail folds. |
| [DE636947](../patents/DE636947.md) | 1936 | Winklhofer, Wanderer Werke Vorm | A single paper holding rail spanning the platen is pivoted on the support frame with a stop, and a handle switches it between a hold-off position and a position pressed against the platen. |
| [JP119719](../patents/JP119719.md) | 1936 | Suzuki, Kuraji | A single rod that moves at every key stroke is shifted by a three-position handle so that its motion works only the vertical-feed mechanism, only the horizontal-feed mechanism, or neither. |
| [DE638139](../patents/DE638139.md) | 1936 | Duemling, Adolf | Three type cylinders on an endless belt run past a fixed vertical platen, each stepped one character along the line, so a new cylinder takes over as the previous one leaves the line. |
| [JP119718](../patents/JP119718.md) | 1936 | Suzuki, Kuraji | A handle-set switching device routes the print-key and spacing-lever motion to either the cylinder rotation (vertical-feed) mechanism or the carriage sideways (horizontal-feed) mechanism, while one line-space key does the opposite motion in each mode. |
| [JP120536](../patents/JP120536.md) | 1936 | Suganuma, Seiichi | A finger-operated knob wheel with a small gear meshing the cylinder's large gear can turn the cylinder by any small amount, while one press of a finger-piece both brakes the knob shaft and frees the feed ratchet's pawl. |
| [DE639956](../patents/DE639956.md) | 1936 | H, Original Checko G M B | Thin rubber insert with corrugations on both sides, offset so the sheet keeps even thickness, placed between platen and paper. |
| [DE639934](../patents/DE639934.md) | 1936 | Stein, Wilhelm | Individual text and image parts are transferred onto rubber partial rollers, which then print the composed form on a partial-roller press. |
| [DE641477](../patents/DE641477.md) | 1937 | Co, Underwood Elliott Fisher | A lifting frame is supported by two removable pins that engage eccentric pins on the platen frame, so the whole lifting frame can be removed and refitted without loosening the platen. |
| [DE589017](../patents/DE589017.md) | 1937 | Winklhofer, Wanderer Werke Vorm | The platen body is a thick-walled or solid light metal cylinder of low elasticity and low sound conduction, with the usual rubber covering. |
| [JP121662](../patents/JP121662.md) | 1937 | Katō, Kenjirō | At the end of travel a base slider swings the sideways-feed rack out of mesh, letting the stored spring force return the carriage, and the rack swing turns the platen one line. |
| [DE644650](../patents/DE644650.md) | 1937 | Winklhofer, Wanderer Werke Vorm | Running bars on the frame guide the platen carriage over its full length, and actuators on the front of the machine move it forward and back with an eccentric guide. |
| [JP122182](../patents/JP122182.md) | 1937 | Hosoya, Shinji | A slide rod driven by the press lever is raised or lowered by a hand lever through a push-pull rod so it engages either the vertical-feed or horizontal-feed member. |
| [DE753696](../patents/DE753696.md) | 1937 | Werke, Mercedes Bueromaschinen | Paper guide plate and punching are set automatically by the carriage's movement between booking cycles. |
| [DE649760](../patents/DE649760.md) | 1937 | Co, Underwood Elliott Fisher | Front and rear adjustable stop supports with inclined and horizontal surfaces on the frame limit stroke both up and down while the base position stays fixed. |
| [DE650261](../patents/DE650261.md) | 1937 | Werke, Mercedes Bueromaschinen | The lower and upper pressure members pivot on a common axis under one spring, so they press the collecting sheet at its edges against the platen automatically. |
| [DE650436](../patents/DE650436.md) | 1937 |  | The pressure roller is made of sponge rubber, which conforms to tape and base irregularities without adjustment and is softer than the gelatin. |
| [DE651736](../patents/DE651736.md) | 1937 | Glaeser, Heinrich | A stationary half ring fits in a wide platen groove and gives a lower-edge trigger that engages a lock or signal. |
| [DE637124](../patents/DE637124.md) | 1937 | Glaeser, Heinrich | One or more ring grooves within the font width of the platen let the lower margin button work on any sheet width. |
| [DE653587](../patents/DE653587.md) | 1937 | Winklhofer, Wanderer Werke Vorm | Mount the line-height pointer and ribbon lifter on parts independent of the in-and-out adjustment of the platen carriage, so they keep a constant distance from the type guide. |
| [DE653658](../patents/DE653658.md) | 1937 | Winklhofer, Wanderer Werke Vorm | Both line indicator bars on a common carrier follow the platen shift and can be moved by one handle between a close-fitting position and a slightly raised position, pressing a card against the platen when needed. |
| [JP125927](../patents/JP125927.md) | 1938 | Miwa, Mataichi | A feed pawl on a spring swing lever with a limiter stops one spacing per print, and the return spring is anchored to the limiter so its tension scales with spacing. |
| [DE656742](../patents/DE656742.md) | 1938 | Ebert, Hellmut | A protector of two fabric sheets glued together, with a stiffening impregnation or hard elastic strips along the face and corners, gives the rigidity needed to slide over the platen. |
| [DE657021](../patents/DE657021.md) | 1938 | Benzing, Hugo | The platen core tube has only metal stub axles running in sound-absorbing bearing bushes, so the vibration is damped at the bearings and does not reach the frame. |
| [DE659966](../patents/DE659966.md) | 1938 | Stertz, Walter | A carrier fixed to the type guide holds the ink-cushion line wheel so the underline falls at the usual distance without platen adjustment and without fouling the ribbon. |
| [DE660882](../patents/DE660882.md) | 1938 | A, Olympia Bueromaschinenwerke | Hollow light-metal support cylinder with transverse walls forming closed air cells, with a regular corrugated outer surface under the usual rubber covering. |
| [JP127819](../patents/JP127819.md) | 1938 | Hosoya, Shinji | One handle turns a disc type magazine, a character-table band and pointers together, so the group shown in the window is already under the printing levers and a numbered key prints it. |
| [DE661490](../patents/DE661490.md) | 1938 | Benzing, Hugo | Fingers mount on journals perpendicular to the platen axis with a curved guide, so each finger pivots within a plane parallel to the card path and holds the card on the roller. |
| [JP128089](../patents/JP128089.md) | 1938 | Hosoya, Shinji | A disc-shaped type magazine is turned by a crank in step with a character index pointer and window, and a shift lever slides it forward or back one or two character spaces so that the wanted type sits under one of a few fixed printing levers. |
| [JP127820](../patents/JP127820.md) | 1938 | Hosoya, Shinji | Each press lever drives, in sequence and through separate links, a cam-slot linked type-lifting rod, a delayed printing arm, and a feed linkage that moves the paper cylinder when the lever is released. |
| [JP127896](../patents/JP127896.md) | 1938 | Shimada, Minokichi | An intermediate gear train with a one-way fine ratchet and pawl follows the step-feed drive in one direction only, so the cylinder can be turned back freely by any amount at once, and a knob can disengage it for free turning both ways. |
| [DE665692](../patents/DE665692.md) | 1938 | Kuebart, Fritz | Two independent drivers engage notches on the roller face, each with wedge surfaces so it only drives in one direction. |
| [JP129571](../patents/JP129571.md) | 1938 | Yōtō, Osamu | A single spring pulls a cord that tilts a rocking plate left or right, and its inclined slot switches one of two meshing pinions into the platen frame rack, with a one-way ratchet metering each step. |
| [DE668245](../patents/DE668245.md) | 1938 | U, Torpedo Werke A G Fahrraeder | Each socket pin is adjusted separately through slotted bushings, so the carriage bed can be set parallel to the type point. |
| [DE668246](../patents/DE668246.md) | 1938 | Werke, Mercedes Bueromaschinen | The platen core is a small-diameter metal tube widened at its ends, and the rubber layer is vulcanised over the thinner middle, which gives a thicker rubber layer on the writing area and room for the clutch inside the platen. |
| [DE668821](../patents/DE668821.md) | 1938 | Hell, - Rudolf | The character picture lines are grouped into line groups, one per text line, and recorded by a magnet system with a cutting edge against a rotating roller, so that a whole sheet of text is recorded as lines. |
| [JP129969](../patents/JP129969.md) | 1938 | Miwa, Mataichi | Make the cylinder's sideways feed happen during the printing key's down-stroke while the type lifter is dug into the tray, timed by printing-bar acceleration and cylinder inertia so the feed follows the strike, and keep a fixed pawl on the feed gear during character search. |
| [DE671886](../patents/DE671886.md) | 1939 | Typewriters, L C Smith & Corona | The pawl carrier pivots on an outward hub of the ratchet wheel, bears against the shaft bearing and the line index wheel, and has a fixed part running over the wheel circumference to stop it bending. |
| [JP130157](../patents/JP130157.md) | 1939 | Yōtō, Osamu | A gear meshing with teeth cut inside the platen is driven, under platen spring force, against a spring-loaded toothed sector whose stop position is chosen by a stepped selector plate, and a key-driven lift rod switches the gear between fixed teeth and the sector. |
| [DE673018](../patents/DE673018.md) | 1939 | Werke, Mercedes Bueromaschinen | The line-setting lever is a bracket whose legs carry spring-holding separators that brake the ratchet lever, so the pawl can be moved independently and each position is held. |
| [JP131648](../patents/JP131648.md) | 1939 | Nagata, Toyoji | A key stroke slides a lever and link that turn a small gear, while a notched brake plate and detent release and re-catch a gear so it turns a fixed amount, moving the character-spacing rack by one or two teeth. |
| [DE675363](../patents/DE675363.md) | 1939 | Gardner, Albert Thomas | The crank is hinged at the end of the roller shaft and held by a spring in the axial position when unused, then swung through about 90 degrees into use to turn the platen. |
| [DE675462](../patents/DE675462.md) | 1939 | Leixner, Anton | A typewriter-type printing device on a table, actuated by a foot-operated type lever, records the weave crossings so both hands stay free. |
| [JP131880](../patents/JP131880.md) | 1939 | Katō, Kenjirō | Link the tray locking piece to the horizontal/vertical changeover so that in horizontal writing it holds until the sideways platen feed finishes, and in vertical writing it releases as soon as the type has dropped back into the tray. |
| [DE677518](../patents/DE677518.md) | 1939 | Haberfeld, Erwin O | Soft springy rollers of larger diameter provide friction, while smaller hard rollers coaxial with them carry the spring pressure, so the soft rollers are not over-pressed. |
| [DE677971](../patents/DE677971.md) | 1939 | Appelt, Hans | A positive connection between the feed pawl and the locking roller lifts the roller at the start of line spacing and drops it back automatically at the end, while a friction brake lets the pawl lift out of the ratchet on the return so it travels back idle and the platen is never unsecured. |
| [DE679612](../patents/DE679612.md) | 1939 | Becker, Walther | Paper guide members are pivoted radially away from the platen, with springs and a lever operated by axial movement of the platen knob, so one hand can insert the sheet. |
| [JP133596](../patents/JP133596.md) | 1939 | Kataoka, Kōtarō | One handle's successive movements steady the type drum, raise the held type frame to the platen, then drive a circular-path striker against the back of the type. |
| [DE680664](../patents/DE680664.md) | 1939 | Strauss, Emil | A rigid stop bar with a flat front and a curved back, mounted on resilient legs or springs, supports the type against the rubber roller while the roller only conveys paper. |
| [DE683321](../patents/DE683321.md) | 1939 | Ges, Wanderer Werke Akt | Paper guide means are arranged so sheets can be inserted while keeping the writing line visible; the specific mechanism is cut off in the excerpt. |
| [DE755158](../patents/DE755158.md) | 1939 | Ag, Zeiss Ikon | A coupling with a switching lever and two slots lets the front rail move alone or both rails move together. |
| [JP135900](../patents/JP135900.md) | 1939 | Kandō, Midori | A swing frame worked by the print key alternately meshes a fixed gear (holding the platen still during printing) and a rotary gear (letting the platen spring-feed afterwards) with a large gear on the platen shaft. |
| [DE683818](../patents/DE683818.md) | 1939 | Ag, Mauser Werke | A platen fixed in the paper carriage works with a pressure roller that covers the ribbon ends and draws the ribbon back to the type carrier when the pre-insert device opens. |
| [DE686395](../patents/DE686395.md) | 1940 | Co, Underwood Elliott Fisher | A weak spring, in the form of a thin resilient shield hinged on the lever's nose, presses the lever lightly against its stop in rest position and cushions the start of the line-space stroke. |
| [DE687971](../patents/DE687971.md) | 1940 | Sherman, John Quirk | Feed pins are guided to move radially with respect to the platen, and cams on telescopic tubes select pin groups for different widths. |
| [JP136653](../patents/JP136653.md) | 1940 | Ishii, Shōkichi | A hand-turned compound magazine of many type discs on one shaft presents one row of loose types at a time under a fan of key-driven type bars, each of which picks up and prints its own type. |
| [DE688608](../patents/DE688608.md) | 1940 | Ges, Wanderer Werke Akt | Two handles at the platen ends drive the line index through four-bar linkages in front of the carriage, transmitting motion via a separate bar, usable from either hand. |
| [DE756189](../patents/DE756189.md) | 1940 | Co, Standard Register | A delivery cylinder with pins on a metal tube, driven by a cam disc; a common switch brings either the pin feed or the friction pressure rollers into operation. |
| [DE691974](../patents/DE691974.md) | 1940 | Andreas, Salzberger | An unloaded sensing element is brought into position with a handle and the platen is set on stops, so the paper gap is set without moving the full mass. |
| [DE692775](../patents/DE692775.md) | 1940 | Ge, Triumph Werke Nuernberg Akt | A platen core with longitudinal ribs subdividing transverse-rib cavities damps noise, with core and sheath made separately. |
| [JP140244](../patents/JP140244.md) | 1940 | Katō, Kenjirō | The feed gear sits on a lower plate hung on parallel links from a fixed upper plate, so a key lever slides it straight in and out of mesh with the platen rack against a spring. |
| [DE698093](../patents/DE698093.md) | 1940 | H, Schmidt Arno | Both ends of the carbon sheets are held by holders that are driven in common with the paper web unwinding roller, so the sheets are guided evenly; the holders are supported on the platen and linked to their carriers by sliding joints. |
| [JP141834](../patents/JP141834.md) | 1940 | Katō, Kenjirō | Only the small feed gear is moved up and down: a change-over handle picks one of two counter-rotating driving gears for left or right feed, and a separate key lever lifts it clear of the platen rack while a detent blade is withdrawn. |
| [DE700204](../patents/DE700204.md) | 1940 | H, Hollerith Maschinen Ges M B | The unwinding and winding rollers are driven independently of the platen, and clamps sense the sag of the paper before and after the platen to control their drive. |
| [DE700983](../patents/DE700983.md) | 1941 | A, C Olivetti & C S | A swash plate engages a groove in a disc on the roller so that one motion both rotates the roller and shifts it axially. |
| [DE701017](../patents/DE701017.md) | 1941 | Buschmann, Eugen | An adjustable actuator sets the engagement start of the pawl in the ratchet; the stop returns automatically after setting. |
| [DE701404](../patents/DE701404.md) | 1941 | Ag, Wanderer Werke | Extra support members at platen-axis height press against fixed rails opposite the type pressure, holding the platen rigid in each writing position. |
| [DE701405](../patents/DE701405.md) | 1941 | Ag, Wanderer Werke | An intermediate member holding the line-lock lever up is brought out of action by stops at the end of the forward gear, so the locking roller engages at the right moment. |
| [DE701918](../patents/DE701918.md) | 1941 | Ag, Wanderer Werke | A pretensioned spring acts between an actuator engaging incisions in the support rod and a disc that moves in a limited pin-slot, so brake force stays within small limits regardless of width. |
| [DE705653](../patents/DE705653.md) | 1941 | Ges, Seidel & Naumann Akt | Home-like caps with bent guide tabs are placed on a guide rail to direct the paper under the rail, with rollers and tubes for rotation. |
| [DE706633](../patents/DE706633.md) | 1941 | Co, Ncr | The line-advance drive is tied to the pivoting movement of the paper roller frame through a lifting curve and an indexing arm, so the roller still advances. |
| [DE710083](../patents/DE710083.md) | 1941 | Ges, Seidel & Naumann Akt | Drive the platen forward and back by a toggle (knee) joint powered by a motor, so platen speed is nearly zero at the moment of impression while force is greatest. |
| [DE710133](../patents/DE710133.md) | 1941 | Berrendorf, Franz Josef | The platen pivot sits inside the pawl bearing so the axis distance equals one line spacing, with no separate stop arm, and a spring keeps the pawl on the platen teeth. |
| [DE712359](../patents/DE712359.md) | 1941 | Ag, Wanderer Werke | A self-contained frame on the platen carriage holds a pivoting pawl carrier with a range-setting member and a combined line-space and carriage-return lever. |
| [DE716482](../patents/DE716482.md) | 1942 | Klebs, Friedrich Georg | Arrow keys turn the platen with ratchet wheels mounted directly on the platen shaft, driven by pawls on two rocker shafts, eliminating the gears. |
| [DE765018](../patents/DE765018.md) | 1942 | Co, Underwood Elliott Fisher | Tilt the whole type engine, carriers, casing, carriage support and switching parts as one unit forward and downward by an acute angle about the platen axis, so the line is visible while the types still strike radially. |
| [DE717421](../patents/DE717421.md) | 1942 | Becker, Walther | The rail lift member is placed next to the rear paper guide so the same hand that holds the sheet can operate it. |
| [DE717603](../patents/DE717603.md) | 1942 | Ag, Wanderer Werke | Support rollers on the switchable part rest against stops on the fixed carriage at both end positions, so the platen holds a rigid position against type pressure. |
| [DE719062](../patents/DE719062.md) | 1942 | Werke, Mercedes Bueromaschinen | A universal link lifts all guide rollers with a single handle, and the lower rollers have pressure fingers whose pressure increases when lifted. |
| [JP152547](../patents/JP152547.md) | 1942 | Suzuki, Kuraji | A spring-driven paper cylinder is stepped by a rocking frame whose pawl and pinion release a set angle of gear rotation per stroke, with a sliding lever for hand feed and reverse and an operating plate that frees the cylinder. |
| [DE721383](../patents/DE721383.md) | 1942 | Werke, Mercedes Bueromaschinen | A longitudinally slotted tube with stepped lugs and tabs holds plastic bearing bushes, with a rubber tube over it, so the parts assemble without extra machining. |
| [DE721726](../patents/DE721726.md) | 1942 | Ruediger, Ernst | A stretchable rubber ring with a raised scale slips over the platen knob and is held by its inward edges, so one size fits the knob range. |
| [DE722555](../patents/DE722555.md) | 1942 | Pascher, Leopold Ferdinand | Two semicircular clutch jaws on a hub inside the line index wheel are spread apart by a wedge-shaped bumper against springs, so the wheel couples and releases directly to the platen tube. |
| [DE724442](../patents/DE724442.md) | 1942 | G, Steckborn Kunstseide A | A hollow vertical platen climbs a fixed screw spindle; two-armed levers inside it carry thread jaws that can be squeezed out of engagement by hand to reposition or remove the platen. |
| [DE724443](../patents/DE724443.md) | 1942 | Fernand, Bessiere | The middle platen is smaller in diameter than the side rollers, so the narrow carbon and original webs run on it without contact with the wider web, and a pressure roller drives them together. |
| [DE726475](../patents/DE726475.md) | 1942 | Hegnauer, Ruf Buchhaltung Paul | A second soft resilient rail, bowed toward the platen and clamped on the first rail, holds the sheet already inserted while the next is fed. |
| [DE726476](../patents/DE726476.md) | 1942 | Schueler, Hugo | The platen has a hollow tubular core with a rubber covering, supported evenly, which reduces noise and is cheap to make. |
| [DE728412](../patents/DE728412.md) | 1942 | Guiseppe, Preziosa | A large gear on the platen carrier drives optical and acoustic indicators only when the platen turns in the writing direction, and yields when turned the other way. |
| [DE729012](../patents/DE729012.md) | 1942 | Pascher, Leopold | A locking disc with a hole sits between the line-index wheel and the knob foot; a driver passes through it, so the platen stays freely rotatable until the knob is turned back. |
| [DE879551](../patents/DE879551.md) | 1943 | Corp, Underwood | A toggle lever on the carriage holds the removable platen axle in its open bearing in the self-locking extended position and releases it when bent. |
| [DE735098](../patents/DE735098.md) | 1943 | Miloslav, Bradik | Rotate the platen to each staff line by a foot-pedal lever system acting on a pendulum frame under the machine, with an adjustable stop bar for staves of different widths. |
| [JP159441](../patents/JP159441.md) | 1943 | Kawahara, Kenji | Label each type with its half-width in platen-feed units and turn a feed dial by the sum of the previous and next type's half-widths before each strike. |
| [DE737651](../patents/DE737651.md) | 1943 | Gundlfinger, Karl | A spring-loaded articulated parallelogram guides the web so it stays taut and in line with the platen as the carriage moves. |
| [DE738766](../patents/DE738766.md) | 1943 | Kuhrt, Ernst | A rack in a guide rail is held by friction, and an intermediate pinion on a fixed axis drives the platen in one stroke direction only. |
| [JP162305](../patents/JP162305.md) | 1943 | Nakazono, Sadaji | A limiter of different height beside each type cell stops a riser at a height set by the picked type's width, which sets how many teeth the platen feed works. |
| [DE740296](../patents/DE740296.md) | 1943 | Haberfeld, Erwin O | A guide plate and a strip on the sheet hold the sheet on the platen and show when the last line is reached, keeping the sheet clamped. |
| [DE921387](../patents/DE921387.md) | 1943 | H, Adlerwerke Kleyer Ag | The rails and side walls are cast in one piece with the paper support and rear wall, and platen bearing brackets are adjustable by eccentric pivot screws so the platen sits exactly at the type stop. |
| [DE741954](../patents/DE741954.md) | 1943 | Werke, Mercedes Bueromaschinen | A roof-shaped pressure member, pivoted resiliently near the stop point, presses the inserted sheet to the platen as the carriage moves past. |
| [JP163316](../patents/JP163316.md) | 1943 | Ochiai, Isamu | The feed pawl always moves the drive ratchet one tooth, and a sliding gear meshing with one of several aligned gears of different diameters changes the ratio to the platen. |
| [JP163317](../patents/JP163317.md) | 1943 | Ochiai, Isamu | Same sliding-gear change-speed train as JP163316, with the paired eccentric second-group gears clamped together by spacer discs and screw spindles for easy assembly. |
| [JP163318](../patents/JP163318.md) | 1943 | Ochiai, Isamu | Two opposed racks on a rockable cross bar straddle one one-way drive gear, and a handle rocks the bar to mesh one rack or the other. |
| [DE743866](../patents/DE743866.md) | 1944 | Berrendorf, Josef | The platen is raised into a closed housing and rotated for transport, the foot is set into the housing profile, and the numbers can still be read from above. |
| [DE744472](../patents/DE744472.md) | 1944 | Neumann, Oswald | The selection device also limits paper carriage travel in both directions, so zones can be set and skipped in one operation. |
| [DE745225](../patents/DE745225.md) | 1944 | Hans, Martin | A wedge between the bearing carrier and a sloped frame surface is moved by screw to set the platen in the type-imprint direction, and an eccentric on a slotted screw sets the height, with the parts locking each other after adjustment. |
| [DE909940](../patents/DE909940.md) | 1944 | Corp, Underwood | The electric drive of the carriage elevator is made ineffective automatically when the paper-web adjusting device is swung out and the platen is not in the writing position. |
| [DE749098](../patents/DE749098.md) | 1944 |  | Two ribbons print at once; a plate serves as platen at the control-strip print point, interrupting the pressure roller there; built for postal money-order service. |
| [DE920011](../patents/DE920011.md) | 1944 | Ludwig, Karl | A pinion-driven four-wheel gear chain links transport roller and pressure roller at any spacing, so both sides of the stack move at equal speed. |
| [DE875419](../patents/DE875419.md) | 1945 | Co, Burroughs Adding Machine | A work-slip holder moves in step with the line-switchable roller, grips slips at its rest position, and does two prints in one stroke sequence. |
| [DE843702](../patents/DE843702.md) | 1947 | Anstalt, Renor | A paper guide plate pivots on slides in the carriage side walls with pressure and guide rollers, and lugs on the paper holder lift it against a spring. |
| [DE830658](../patents/DE830658.md) | 1948 | Ag, Anker Werke | The sheet guide is adjustable so the preliminary sheet can be moved from the insertion position to a writing position that wraps it further round the platen, with the pressure rollers moved with it. |
| [DE844457](../patents/DE844457.md) | 1948 | Ag, Triumph Werke Nuernberg | A separate clamping member for each locking lever slides on a guide body so the radial release pressure is taken by the guide body; this allows coupling in any position with weaker springs, and the inclined surface is self-locking. |
| [DE809200](../patents/DE809200.md) | 1948 | Denck, Oscar | Typebar machine with an upright platen turned stepwise by key levers for letter spacing and raised one line space by a second stepping drive at line end, giving normal horizontal lines. |
| [DE871603](../patents/DE871603.md) | 1948 | Colombo, Achille | Group the characters on a few type sectors, each carrying 6 to 9 characters, and print any one by pressing two keys together: a selection key from one small keyboard (which tilts the sector to the wanted character) and an operating key from the other (which swings that sector to the platen). |
| [JP178253](../patents/JP178253.md) | 1948 | Kobayashi, Kaichi | Pack the types loosely round the whole ring with a single follower, and use a plunger with ratchet and cap to print and step in one stroke or step alone. |
| [DE838755](../patents/DE838755.md) | 1948 | Brose, & Co Metallwerk Max | A cam lever with detents sets a stop for the line-shift lever, and the toggle-shaped pawl is held firmly in the platen ratchet during the stroke. |
| [DE806550](../patents/DE806550.md) | 1948 | H, Olympia Werke West G M B | The platen is a support tube with a rubber-coated roller and one or more resilient intermediate layers, such as metal springs, between tube and coating. |
| [DE815560](../patents/DE815560.md) | 1948 | Admi, Nuernberg Fuerther Ind Us | The pawl is attached to the platen swivel arm and disengaged from the switching gear when the platen is raised by two fixed pins: one in an elongated hole and one in a curved path that stops the pawl in both end positions. The platen pivot also serves as the guide pin. |
| [DE900940](../patents/DE900940.md) | 1948 | M, Siemag Vertriebsgesellschaft | A roller freewheel with wedge-surface clamping jaws and a simpler structure couples and uncouples the paper roller from the line-index wheel. |
| [DE829897](../patents/DE829897.md) | 1949 | Lennartz, Walter | A plastic film with a smooth front and a roughened back grips the platen, with a transparent stop flap. |
| [DE818870](../patents/DE818870.md) | 1949 | Bosch, Gmbh Robert | A hump on the desk roller breaks the whiskers off once per revolution, or the electrode is vibrated to fling them away. |
| [DE836042](../patents/DE836042.md) | 1949 | W, Kuester & Co K G | Lower the typebar bearing until the short arm just clears the base plate, put the key lever bearing close to and below it, and tilt the key planes at rest so they swing evenly about horizontal. |
| [JP366048](../patents/JP366048.md) | 1949 | Nakata, Tōzaburō | A turnable disc with radial wedge spacers forming type-wide recesses holds several tiers of types, clamped by a notched-rib holding plate with index characters on top. |

| [JP366260](../patents/JP366260.md) | 1949 | Nakata, Tōzaburō | A plate turning on the swinging type shaft is linked to a feed-pawl lever, so every swing of the shaft turns the plate and steps the platen rack one space. |
| [DE801638](../patents/DE801638.md) | 1949 | P, Gossen & Co G M B H | A roller freewheel lets the platen turn freely; pressure pieces hold the line-space ratchet at a fixed engagement when the paper is set. |
| [JP180679](../patents/JP180679.md) | 1949 | Kataoka, Kōtarō | One push-pull knob rod sets both the feed-pawl gap and the push-back travel between two values, with separate striking levers for feed, push-back and printing. |
| [JP367759](../patents/JP367759.md) | 1949 | Nakata, Tōzaburō | One lever links the printing shaft to an L-lever with a catch pawl on the platen rack, so each print stroke steps the platen one tooth. |
| [JP182442](../patents/JP182442.md) | 1949 | Kataoka, Kōtarō | A press key swings a cam that lifts a spring-lowered sector gear into the platen's toothed bar, with an adjustable disc limiting how far it turns back. |
| [JP182406](../patents/JP182406.md) | 1949 | Kataoka, Kōtarō | A spring-loaded swinging lever carries a claw at its tip that is cammed into the rack teeth by the back-feed operating lever. |
| [DE835890](../patents/DE835890.md) | 1950 | H, Powers G M B | The platen is coupled to an actuator only during the adjustment movement, and the travel is set by a selected limit stop, so the next sheet can be pre-set while the machine labels the current one. |
| [DE821795](../patents/DE821795.md) | 1950 | Schwabenbauer, Franz | Transparent plastic guide plates with rounded edges snap onto the holder bracket so stiff card corners slide over the curves back to the platen. |
| [DE855411](../patents/DE855411.md) | 1950 | Thiel, Hans | A special rubberised brush rubs a chemical softening paste into the roller so loosened dirt is taken up and the paste softens the rubber to about 2 mm depth. |
| [DE968266](../patents/DE968266.md) | 1950 | Ab, Original Odhner | A feed hook pivoted on a feed crank engages or is blocked from a return wheel by a cam, so one feed member works for single steps and another adds the two-step or multi-step feed. |
| [DE844908](../patents/DE844908.md) | 1950 | Glatz, Paul Albert | The typewriter prints mirror characters directly: its type carriers carry mirror-image types (or readable types arranged to print mirror images), a switch reverses the platen rotation for the line feed, and mirror reading devices at the type stop positions show a correct image for checking. |
